[Profiles] Move views/profiles unit and interactive UI tests

The unit tests and interactive UI tests in
chrome/browser/ui/views/profiles/ were listed directly in the
monolithic //chrome/test:unit_tests and :interactive_ui_tests targets,
far from the code they exercise. Move them into new :unit_tests and
:interactive_ui_tests source_sets in
//chrome/browser/ui/views/profiles/BUILD.gn, keeping the original
platform conditions, and depend on them from //chrome/test.

Also drop the //chrome/browser/ui/views/profiles:* deps from
//chrome/test that only these tests used. The interactive UI test
target disables -Wexit-time-destructors to match chrome/test.

No behavior change. The set of tests is identical before and after, as
compared with --gtest_list_tests on Linux and ChromeOS. gn check passes
on Linux, ChromeOS, Windows, and Mac, and the new targets compile on
all four.

Bug: 521960574
Test: gn check and build tests on Linux, ChromeOS, Win, and Mac

[Extensions] Remove return value from alarms.clearAll()

Prior to this commit, every call to alarms.clearAll() returns
Promise<true>. This is inconsistent with broader web convention to
return undefined for calls clearing data structures. This CL makes
alarms.clearAll() return Promise<undefined> aligning Chromium with
currently released Safari, Firefox 157+ and agreed upon behavior in WECG
meeting. This change has a kill-switch in case of compatibility
concerns.

Discussion in WECG: https://github.com/w3c/webextensions/issues/1055

Bug: 541711725

[Views] Move views/frame interactive_ui_tests to frame target

The interactive UI tests in chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/ were listed
directly in the monolithic //chrome/test:interactive_ui_tests target,
far from the code they exercise. Move them into a new
:interactive_ui_tests source_set in
//chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/BUILD.gn and depend on it from
//chrome/test. This completes the views/frame migration started by the
browser_tests and unit_tests moves.

Also drop the //chrome/browser/ui/views/frame:* deps from
//chrome/test:interactive_ui_tests that only these tests used, and fix
a -Wsign-compare warning in browser_view_focus_interactive_uitest.cc
that chrome/test did not surface.

No behavior change. The set of tests is identical before and after, as
compared with --gtest_list_tests on Linux (5560 interactive_ui_tests)
and ChromeOS (5418 interactive_ui_tests). gn check passes on Linux,
ChromeOS, Windows, and Mac, and the new target compiles on all four.

Webium: Defer WebUIToolbarUI initialization until location bar is ready

When profile prewarming (`WebUIReloadButtonProfilePrewarming` and
`WebUIReloadButtonPrewarmWebUIPreNavigate`) is enabled, the pre-created
WebContents navigates to chrome://webui-toolbar.top-chrome in the
background and usually finishes loading before the first browser window
is built.

Previously, `WebUIToolbarWebView`'s constructor synchronously called
`WebUIToolbarUI::Init()` if the pre-created WebContents had already
committed and finished loading. However, `ToolbarView::Init()`
constructs `WebUIToolbarWebView` before it calls
`WebUILocationBar::Init()`, which is what creates the
`OmniboxController`, so `GetOmniboxController()` returned nullptr.

`WebUIToolbarUI` records `omnibox_controller_` only once in `Init()`,
and `Init()` cannot run again, so the controller stayed null for the
lifetime of the window. `CreatePageHandler()` also used a null
controller to mean "Init() has not run yet", so it parked the searchbox
`PageHandler` receiver forever, leaving the omnibox's searchbox backend
disconnected.

A second path had the same result: if the prewarmed navigation had
committed but was still loading, the constructor skipped Init(), and
since `DidFinishNavigation` had already fired before the observer was
attached, Init() never ran.

This CL removes the synchronous Init() from the `WebUIToolbarWebView`
constructor. If the pre-created contents have committed (loading or
not), init is marked as pending instead. It adds
`WebUIToolbarWebView::MaybeInitWebUI()`, called from
`ToolbarView::Init()` after the location bar is initialized, to run any
pending init. `DidFinishNavigation()` likewise defers until the toolbar
is ready. Both paths share `InitWebUI()`, which is a no-op if the UI is
already initialized.

Bug: 563226016
Change-Id: I288471a677e7e0cc751f02f840ec52ac484854d5

[Actor] Add Actor.Notification.TimeBetweenWorklogUpdates metric

Records the elapsed time in milliseconds between consecutive step
progress (worklog) updates on an active Actor task notification on
Android.

The metric tracks intervals per task ID in ActorNotificationService when
the notification is updated with step progress while the task is in a
running state, and resets tracking when a task completes or is paused so
that intervals are not measured across task boundaries or idle periods.

TAG=agy
CONV=0af19be5-4de4-4b3b-8c97-b6d10a3e3d6e

Bug: 556842081, 559173223, 567165855

[Actor] Remove default ok button from preparing snackbar

Snackbar.make() sets a default 'OK' action for TYPE_PERSISTENT, and Snackbar.setAction() does not accept a @Nullable action string under @NullMarked. Switch the preparing snackbar to TYPE_NOTIFICATION with a 2x timeout duration so it displays without an action button while dismissal remains managed by ActorExternalTriggerSnackbarController.

Bug: 559173086, 567165855

[MultipleMediaNotifs] Promote fallback tab on stopListenerService()

When MediaNotificationController.stopListenerService() detaches an
active foreground controller (stopForeground(STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE))
while another tab still needs the shared service (isServiceNeeded() is
true), immediately call MediaNotificationManager.handleFallbackOnStop()
so the fallback playing tab takes over FGS ownership without waiting for
delayed notification hiding.

Bug: 522397811

[Views] Move autofill/popup tests to popup targets

The browsertests, unittests, and mock/test-api files in
chrome/browser/ui/views/autofill/popup/ were listed directly in the
monolithic //chrome/test:browser_tests and //chrome/test:unit_tests
targets, far from the code they exercise. Move them into new
:test_support, :browser_tests, and :unit_tests source_set targets in
//chrome/browser/ui/views/autofill/popup/BUILD.gn, and depend on them
from //chrome/test (and //chrome/browser/ui/views/passwords:unit_tests
for :test_support).

views/autofill/popup builds with -Wexit-time-destructors, which
chrome/test disables. Fix the exit-time destructor warnings in
popup_cell_utils_unittest.cc, popup_row_view_unittest.cc, and
popup_view_views_unittest.cc using getter functions and std::to_array.
Keep no_exit_time_destructors on :browser_tests for
popup_row_factory_utils_browsertest.cc, as it will take a larger
refactor to fix these.

No behavior change. The set of tests is identical before and after, as
compared with --gtest_list_tests on Linux (38040 browser_tests, 33393
unit_tests) and ChromeOS (48344 browser_tests, 48912 unit_tests). gn
check passes and unit_tests and browser_tests compile on Linux,
ChromeOS, Windows, and Mac.

Bug: 521960574
Test: gn check and build tests on Linux, ChromeOS, Win, and Mac

Migrate MLInstallabilityPromoter to TabFeatures

Convert MLInstallabilityPromoter from WebContentsUserData to
UnownedUserData, and move its instantiation and ownership from
TabHelpers::AttachTabHelpers to Android and Desktop TabFeatures. In
Desktop TabFeatures::WillDiscardContents, reset and recreate the
promoter for the new WebContents.

Initializing AppBannerManager before MLInstallabilityPromoter means
AppBannerManager's InstallableTask (which fetches web page metadata over
Mojo before fetching the manifest) now runs first on DidFinishLoad. If
the primary main frame's renderer exits while GetWebPageMetadata is in
flight (e.g. during a RetainedWebContents tab discard, which kills the
renderer without destroying the WebContents), advancing to FetchManifest
previously called GetManifest() on a non-live RenderFrameHost and hit
DCHECK(IsRenderFrameLive()). Check that the WebContents and its primary
main frame are still live in InstallableDataFetcher::FetchManifest and
FetchWebPageMetadata, returning RENDERER_EXITING otherwise.

[🌺] Revert "[Android] Cache DeviceInfo in test builds"

This reverts commit d7d74add46d2971946a3ade2a5b990e3e30dfd4a.

Reason for revert: consistently fails android-desktop-x64-rel.

BookmarksApiEventsTest#*, BookmarksApiTest#Bookmarks,
GlicSidePanelCoordinatorDesktopAndroidBrowserTest#*, and
DownloadExtensionTest#DownloadsShowFunction has failed in 39/39 recent
builds of android-desktop-x64-rel, every attempt in every one of them.
This is not flake: it reproduces on every run, so it blocks the CQ for
every unrelated CL that touches this suite. The regression appears at
refs/heads/main@{#1709361}.

Example failing run:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-desktop-x64-rel-15-tests/23058

Caching DeviceInfo.sInstance on first access in test builds caches
mIDeviceInfo.isDesktop = false before CommandLine is populated with
--force-desktop-android on Android 15 tablet emulators (which lack
PackageManager.FEATURE_PC). This causes 15 android_browsertests across
BookmarksApiEventsTest, BookmarksApiTest,
GlicSidePanelCoordinatorDesktopAndroidBrowserTest, and
DownloadExtensionTest to fail deterministically starting at #780879
(1709361, vs passing at #780880 at 1709360) on chromium/try/android-
desktop-x64-rel and in CI build #23058 on chromium/ci/android-
desktop-x64-rel-15-tests.

Reverting to green the tree first. Please re-land once the failure is
understood and covered; this revert is about restoring CQ throughput,
not about the merit of the change.

Original change's description:
> [Android] Cache DeviceInfo in test builds
>
> DeviceInfo.getInstance() rebuilt the object on every call in test
> builds so that mocked inputs were not defeated by caching. Each rebuild
> calls PackageManager.getSystemAvailableFeatures(), which costs two
> synchronous binder calls, and isDesktop() is on hot paths such as
> toolbar invalidation. In traces this was ~11% of main-thread CPU in
> Desktop instrumentation tests.
>
> Cache the instance in tests as well. Invalidate it via
> ResettersForTesting between tests, from the *ForTesting setters, and
> from OverrideContextWrapperTestRule when it changes system features.
>
> Bug: 562154154, 568001105
> Change-Id: If066e6f1a89f333e5efd495e2c529404aa10718a
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8466502
> Reviewed-by: Andrew Grieve <agrieve@chromium.org>
> Commit-Queue: Henrique Nakashima <hnakashima@chromium.org>
> Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1709361}

Bug: 562154154, 568001105

Ash: Migrate unmanaged reporting upload test off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

ReportingServerConnectorTest now waits for the unmanaged device's upload
request before checking that it carries no management token. Queuing the
records does not mean the request is ready: its payload is built on a
worker thread first. Waiting for the pending request lets the test check
its headers without draining unrelated tasks.

Bug: b:506794349
Test: CQ

Revert CHECK to DCHECK in ReadMetadataForDatabaseName

Revert the CHECK macro in ReadMetadataForDatabaseName back to a DCHECK
to prevent crashes in production builds when triggered. A CHECK-
exclusion comment has been added above the check until we are confident
it will not be triggered.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/4a60d5cb2f800000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

Ash: Migrate UserAddingScreenTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

UserAddingScreenTest now waits for the add-user screen to be shown
before requesting screen lock. Reuse the existing screen-shown helper
so the regression test reaches the add-user flow before interrupting
it, without draining unrelated tasks.

Ash: Migrate AccessibilityHandlerTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

AccessibilityHandlerTest enables the settings handler before testing
dictation updates and startup sound. That call registers the speech
installer observer and sends the initial settings data synchronously,
so setup does not need to drain pending tasks before continuing.

Convert null byte CHECK for cache response header values to DCHECK

Revert the CHECK for null bytes in cache response header values back to
a DCHECK_EQ with a CHECK-exclusion comment to prevent crashes when
reading cached header values.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/0ff00cd008400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

tree
cb4442721b571e677280d807c3427c77f1b78623
parent
2a115f6289ae660c6f34d96e2037a21a4cbb3699 [ diff ] 
blink: Disable LSan for static QualifiedName allocations

Static QualifiedNameImpl objects (e.g. in html_names::Init()) are
process-lifetime singletons with refcounting disabled. Unlike
StringImpl::CreateStatic, QualifiedNameImpl::Create lacked LSan
annotations, causing LSan to report false-positive leaks on renderer
exit when internal cache pointers are not scanned as roots.

Scope static QualifiedNameImpl allocations with
LEAK_SANITIZER_DISABLED_SCOPE, matching StringImpl::CreateStatic.
Dynamic allocations remain fully tracked.

Bug: 567815373
Test: blink_unittests --gtest_filter=QualifiedNameTest.*

[ log ]

tree
201441c6b2d416c399dfc293b59ee284368ccc0b
parent
51853d65adfff89a2475366184412fbe3790a8da [ diff ] 
Avoid _CollectDebugData on chrome://gpu Dawn Info wait timeout

When cls.tab.action_runner.WaitForJavaScriptCondition() times out inside
Telemetry's InspectorBackend, Telemetry invokes
browser_backend._CollectDebugData() (capturing a screenshot, system
logs, and running crashpad_database_util 3 times) before raising
py_utils.TimeoutException. In _RetrieveAboutGpu(), this adds ~22-25s of
overhead on top of every 15s Dawn Info timeout (~11.5m across a Debug
shard).

Poll EvaluateJavaScript via py_utils.WaitFor instead of
WaitForJavaScriptCondition so that timeouts do not trigger heavy debug
data collection, and skip waiting for Dawn Info when --disable-gpu is in
browser_args.

Cache chrome://gpu content by browser_args across browser restarts
📋 Metadata
commit
51853d65adfff89a2475366184412fbe3790a8da
[ log ]
author
Jonathan Ross <jonross@chromium.org>
Fri Oct 02 04:00:29 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Oct 02 04:00:29 2026
tree
d05acb0df51989141e2c010f43c715a5d6dc861d
parent
034983c0e50497c7772b42484185152c786e6b81 [ diff ] 
Cache chrome://gpu content by browser_args across browser restarts

GPU integration tests run in alphabetical order and frequently ping-pong
between a small number of unique browser_args sets (e.g. trace_test has
62 args_differ transitions for only 20 unique browser_args sets; pixel
has 27-29 transitions for 9-11 unique sets). Previously,
_RetrieveAboutGpu() only avoided re-fetching when consecutive tests used
identical browser_args, and _RestartBrowser() did not clear
_args_changed_this_browser_start.

Cache _about_gpu_content and _test_that_started_browser by
frozenset(browser_args) so _RetrieveAboutGpu() only navigates to
chrome://gpu once per unique browser_args set on a shard, and clear
_args_changed_this_browser_start in _RestartBrowser().

903eab7995bf5a817a23e493d1687b2910ed04c9 [ diff ] 
[TabUI] Consolidate MultiThumbnailCardProvider paints per fetch

After GM3 tab group colors launched, MultiThumbnailFetcher already
resolves placeholder, text, and ghost illustration colors per fetch from
MultiThumbnailMetadata. However, MultiThumbnailCardProvider still
retained legacy pre-GM3 paints (mEmptyThumbnailPaint,
mThumbnailFramePaint, and setMiniThumbnailPlaceholderColor) that were
never drawn on mCanvas, along with duplicate mSelected* paints/drawables
and an onTabModelChanged observer that mutated shared state on model
switches.

Consolidate into single paint and mutated ghost-drawable instances
configured at draw time from the per-fetch resolved colors (including
favicon background color from MultiThumbnailMetadata#isIncognito), and
remove onTabModelChanged along with the dead pre-GM3 placeholder/frame
helpers and duplicate TabCardThemeUtil methods. Also simplify the
MultiThumbnailFetcher draw pipeline by removing the obsolete lastFavicon
double-callback guard, no-op transparent canvas clear, per-slot
PorterDuffXfermode allocations, and redundant favicon fetches for acting
tabs.

NO_IFTTT=getTabGridCardDividerTintColor had no matching entry in semantic_colors_dynamic.xml

[TabUI] Use tabGroupId in MultiThumbnailMetadata and avoid JNI calls

MultiThumbnailCardProvider previously resolved live tab group thumbnails
indirectly by taking a representative member tabId in
MultiThumbnailMetadata and calling TabModel#getRelatedTabList(tabId).
Pass the group's Token (tabGroupId) in MultiThumbnailMetadata so
MultiThumbnailCardProvider queries TabModel#getTabsInGroup(tabGroupId)
directly, and add dedicated factory methods for single tabs, live tab
groups, and SavedTabGroups.

Additionally, remove ThumbnailItemMetadata and getThumbnailItems, which
previously called Tab#getUrl() across JNI and allocated wrapper objects
for every tab in the group. Tab#getUrl() is now only called for the up
to 4 tabs that are actually rendered on the card.

tree
fb743f98ff6e4f57f45d039ea87e28a3de5fbdcf
parent
31fe4b17a054c2aa0e3f043206be7b053823aa16 [ diff ] 
Interpolate nested animating container children in parent space

When a nested container (such as a tab group) shifts in its parent
while simultaneously expanding or contracting to accommodate a moved
tab, independent integer rounding (base::ClampFloor(x + 0.5f) in
gfx::Tween::RectValueBetween) on opposite-sign half-integer offsets
can round in the same positive direction and cause +/-1px screen-space
jitter on stationary children.

Introduce a lightweight HostAnimationOffsets helper that translates
child start and target bounds into the parent container's coordinate
space before interpolating, then subtracts the host's current
interpolated offset. When the host is not animated by a parent
TabCollectionAnimatingLayoutManager, the offsets default to zero
vectors and reduce directly to standard local interpolation.

tree
f962fe9fe049e0a797227d6c2eab3f757e37886c
parent
4a0419af0da984b5aafa49346a1b1d9f2646873e [ diff ] 
settings: Fix RTL search hint misalignment after side UI resize

In single-column settings (tablet portrait), the search query EditText
lives in the toolbar. With an RTL UI, opening or closing the side UI
(vertical tabs, side panel) left the hint/text either clipped on the
right or shifted left by the amount the toolbar had been resized.

Root cause: a single-line EditText lays its text out in a very wide
(1048576px) layout and positions RTL text by scrolling to scrollX =
VERY_WIDE - width. TextView only re-syncs scrollX after a measure pass.
The side UI resize animates the tab content view and all of its
descendants with a ChangeBounds transition, which calls
setLeftTopRightBottom() on each view without measuring it. The EditText
therefore ends up with a new width but the scrollX computed for the old
width, and the text is drawn at the wrong offset. LTR text is unaffected
because its scrollX is always 0.

Fix: introduce ResizeAwareEditText, which overrides onSizeChanged() and
calls bringPointIntoView() whenever the width changes, so the scroll
position is re-derived from the new bounds even when the resize bypasses
measurement. Use it for the settings search query field.

Add a regression test that resizes the settings container via a
ChangeBounds transition in RTL and verifies the query field's scroll
position matches its width. The test fails without the fix.

Bug: 568207264, 521895796
Test: SettingsPageTest

tree
e14ce26d8c1a22ab4904e94dcf2678a31e916212
parent
8ee43853d8c5e50ebeebf40bbda7b9203a139aaf [ diff ] 
[WebUI LLBC] Open Page Info on right-click release, not contextmenu

The WebUI location icon forwarded every `contextmenu` event to
onClick_() to open the Page Info bubble. That differs from native
LocationIconView in a few ways:

- Timing: on Linux and Mac, `contextmenu` fires on mouse press, but
  the native button opens the bubble on release (ButtonController::
  OnMouseReleased). Pressing on the chip and releasing elsewhere
  still opened the bubble, while native cancels the click when
  HitTestPoint fails. `auxclick` fixes both.
- Touch long-press: `contextmenu` fires on long-press, so Page Info
  opened. Native LocationIconView::IsTriggerableEvent rejects every
  gesture except kGestureTap.
- Keyboard context-menu key (Shift+F10 / Menu key): this opened Page
  Info. Native does nothing because LocationIconView has no
  ContextMenuController.

This CL handles right-click in an `auxclick` listener (button 2),
which fires on release and only when press and release land on the
chip. `contextmenu` is now only preventDefault()-ed. Middle-click
is still excluded, so paste-and-go keeps working. This is the same
pattern ContentSettingIconElement uses.

[EdgeToEdge] Clarify bottom-only E2E support on NativePage

This is a trivial/mechanical renaming change.

NativePage#supportsEdgeToEdge() and
EdgeToEdgeUtils#isNativeTabDrawingToEdge() only check whether a native
page supports drawing to the bottom edge, while top edge-to-edge is
tracked separately via NativePage#supportsEdgeToEdgeOnTop().

Rename NativePage#supportsEdgeToEdge() to supportsEdgeToEdgeOnBottom()
and EdgeToEdgeUtils#isNativeTabDrawingToEdge() to
isNativeTabDrawingToBottomEdge() across implementations and unit tests
to clearly distinguish bottom vs. top edge-to-edge support.

Revert CHECK back to DCHECK in SearchSessionMetricsManager::OnLaunch

Revert CHECK(session_active_) in OnLaunch back to DCHECK. In release
builds, OnLaunch could be called when session_active_ is false,
triggering a fatal CHECK crash. Using DCHECK avoids crashing in
production builds while maintaining debug assertions.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/febbdc6170400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

Revert "Part 2 - Preload Dolby Vision decoder library in GPU process"

This reverts commit 1fb89696729df0e5140e5ea5d53e633cf17450ab.

Reason for revert: Product has decided not to support DV for clear content, so these changes aren't needed. https://crbug.com/350869451#comment8

Original change's description:
> Part 2 - Preload Dolby Vision decoder library in GPU process
>
> As in CL 5373878 for audio decoders, this CL extends the same
> package-locator/runtime-loading flow to video codecs, with Dolby Vision
> as the first codec.
>
> The Dolby Vision decoder MFT is used in the GPU process for clear
> (unencrypted) Dolby Vision playback. Because the GPU process is
> sandboxed after startup, the MFT must be located and loaded before
> sandboxing; otherwise it cannot be loaded later.
>
> Bug: 350869451

parent
afa7d558ed441551b6b6c398d9756882eef302a3 [ diff ] 
Replace std::string globals in chrome/browser/ash tests

Namespace-scope std::string constants, and constexpr tables of structs
with std::string fields, only avoid an exit-time destructor while every
literal fits in the short-string buffer (10 chars on 32-bit ARM, 22 on
64-bit). Several here already exceed the 32-bit limit, and the rest
are one edit away from it.

- Use constexpr char/char16_t arrays for string constants.
- Use std::string_view fields so parameter tables can stay constexpr.
- Move tables that need owning strings (kiosk WiFi services) into the
  fixture, and inline single-use objects (PolicyNamespace).

TAG=agy
CONV=24fb0bac-49f1-4a9f-9459-e507cfd2dbfd

[Discarding] Fast-kill dedicated main frame and OOPIF processes

Previously, AttemptFastKillForDiscard() only skipped unload handlers on
ChromeOS urgent discards, and did not fast-kill dedicated out-of-process
iframe (OOPIF) renderer processes.

This CL:
1. Allows AttemptFastKillForDiscard() to retry FastShutdownIfPossible()
   with skip_unload_handlers = true on URGENT discards across all
   platforms when features::kWebContentsDiscard is enabled (provided no
   frame in the tab has a beforeunload handler). Non-urgent discards
   preserve legacy behavior by skipping up-front fast-kill when unload
   handlers are present so they can run during DiscardFrame().
2. Iterates over all unique subframe RenderProcessHosts and attempts
   fast-shutdown for dedicated OOPIF processes (using
   use_outermost_main_frame_check = true) before fast-killing the main
   frame process.
3. Updates TabManagerTest.UrgentFastShutdownWithUnloadHandler and adds
   TabManagerTest.ProactiveFastShutdownWithUnloadHandler and
   TabManagerTest.UrgentFastShutdownWithOOPIF.

TAG=agy
CONV=c6cf1d19-f00a-41a1-b753-3c77a936c2f0

08d11c20009e679e1632582d680388518e46a965 [ diff ] 
[omnibox] Use ChromeAutocompleteProviderClient in SearchProvider tests

Remove the local TestAutocompleteProviderClient subclass from
search_provider_unittest.cc and use ChromeAutocompleteProviderClient
directly:
- Its GetURLLoaderFactory() override was unused since SearchProvider and
  ZeroSuggestProvider use RemoteSuggestionsServiceFactory, which is
  already wired to test_url_loader_factory_.
- Configure IsUrlDataCollectionActive() via the underlying
  unified_consent::prefs::kUrlKeyedAnonymizedDataCollectionEnabled pref
  on the TestingProfile rather than overriding the method.

Note: Normally, I would argue that the 2nd change here is bad. Unit tests should be encapsulated and overriding IsUrlDataCollectionActive() in a fake is a good way to do this. _However_, the tests here are really integration tests not unit tests, as they uses the real ChromeAutocompleteProviderClient, real profile service, real history service, and more. So I think it's arguably better to match that and directly change the pref that drives the URL collection state.

Fixed: 567549632

[Omnibox] Add virtual focus Tab navigation for contextual chips

Adds virtual focus Tab/Shift-Tab navigation and Enter/Space activation
for #currentTabChip and #lensSearchChip in the WebUI Omnibox popup via a
new `SelectionLineState.kFocusedButtonContextualChip` state.

Pressing Enter on the current tab chip after tabbing through suggestions
navigated to the AI Mode SRP instead of opening the AIM popup like a
click does. C++ ignored the default selection WebUI sends when virtual
focus leaves the match list, so the edit model stayed on the last
focused search suggestion. To fix this:
- `SearchboxHandler::SetPopupSelection` now resets the edit model
  selection and temporary text when virtual focus leaves the match list.
- `WebuiOmniboxHandler::AddTabContext` now uses
  `AimActivation::kContextMenu` so adding tab context always opens the
  AIM popup.

http://screencast/cast/NjQ4OTMzMDk5NzUyNjUyOHxhNzM1MTQ2MS1hMw

tree
68c2f7947f29205c6555b87bb560950ba538251a
parent
47af9db28657061a02ea05d1b0c884d00cbf9dc6 [ diff ] 
[Enterprise] Attribute URL filtering events for uncommitted navigations

If a navigation matched a URL filtering rule without triggering an
interstitial, but ended without committing (download, 204, stopped or
replaced navigation, tab closed), its event was reported with the tab's
last committed URL and, with EnterpriseTabTitleReporting enabled, that
page's title. When the verdict arrived after the navigation finished,
it was also stored on the page still in the tab and its data protection
settings were applied there.

DataProtectionNavigationObserver now tracks the navigation URL across
redirects. A navigation that finishes without committing reports that
URL with no tab title and never applies its verdict, whether the
verdict arrives before or after DidFinishNavigation(). The destructor,
which handles navigations that never finish, does the same. The
late-verdict case no longer records
Enterprise.DelayedReportingInterstitial.* samples.

Both committed and uncommitted navigations now report the URL the
real-time lookup ran on (GetOriginalUrl()), so a reader mode navigation
reports the article URL instead of the chrome-distiller:// URL. For
other URLs the reported URL is unchanged.

Tests:
- Unit tests for the early-verdict, late-verdict and destroyed
  mid-navigation cases, and for committed and uncommitted reader mode
  navigations.
- New NoContentNavigation browser test (204 response).
- FallbackFlushNavigation expects the pending URL and no title.
- PrivacyIncognitoNavigation: drop the incognito SetTestingFactoryAndUse
  call, which was a no-op because the lookup service factory is
  original-profile-only. Assert instead that the incognito profile has
  no lookup service and that the regular profile's is never called.

Follow-up to crrev.com/c/8394870.

ARM, 22 on 64-bit), and other non-trivial statics never do.

- Return the dummy WebUI URL by value instead of a static GURL.

FSA: Check change source initialization before notifying observers

Require initialization to be pending or to have succeeded before
notifying observers of file or OS usage changes. Use NotFatalUntil::M160
for the new checks to collect non-fatal diagnostics in official builds
before enforcing the invariant. Stop notification delivery if
initialization has not started or has failed.

Cover both NotifyOfChange overloads and usage changes before, during and
after initialization, including failure. Initialize fake sources
explicitly in existing notification and overlapping-scope tests where
observation setup does not initialize the source being used.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
Fix map key mismatch in ThirdPartyMetricsObserver::GetThirdPartyInfo
📋 Metadata
commit
f15d88ac0bfd332232d57063066d8166c377ca59
[ log ]
author
Chris Davis <chrdavis@microsoft.com>
Fri Oct 02 02:13:52 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Oct 02 02:13:52 2026
tree
ae08db9cd761026993d6e888cfb82e44dad3c77e
parent
3cb0e686e92bb3b57946242eb22db8448b66b7c1 [ diff ] 
Fix map key mismatch in ThirdPartyMetricsObserver::GetThirdPartyInfo

ThirdPartyMetricsObserver keeps a ThirdPartyInfo per third party in
all_third_party_info_, keyed by the third party's representative URL,
scheme://eTLD+1/ (or scheme://host/ when there is no registrable
domain). GetThirdPartyInfo() looked up the representative URL, but on a
miss it inserted the new entry under the full |url| instead. The lookup
therefore only succeeded once the third party had been seen at exactly
its root URL; otherwise every distinct full URL, including path and
query, got its own entry instead of sharing one per third party.

This skewed the metrics computed from the map:
- PageLoad.Clients.ThirdParty.Origins.CookieRead2 was over-reported. It
  counted distinct third-party URLs that read cookies rather than
  distinct third parties whenever a third party read cookies from more
  than one URL (different paths, queries or subdomains).
- The ThirdPartyAccessAndActivation use counter was under-reported. It
  only fired when the user activation and the cookie or storage access
  were attributed to the same URL, so e.g. not for an activated iframe
  at https://a.com/widget whose cookies are then read by a request to
  https://a.com/api.
- The map's 1000-entry cap could be exhausted by the URLs of just a few
  third parties (e.g. with cache-busting query strings). After that,
  further third parties were dropped from CookieRead2 and from the
  ThirdPartyAccess, ThirdPartyActivation and
  ThirdPartyAccessAndActivation use counters.

The mismatch was introduced by b535aa4a50b9 ("Adding reporting for third
party content that accesses storage."), which moved this lookup into
GetThirdPartyInfo(); before it, entries were inserted under the
representative URL.

This change inserts new entries under the same key that is looked up. It
also iterates the map by const reference in RecordMetrics() instead of
copying every entry.

Keying by full URL also made the map larger than intended, since each
entry kept a copy of a full URL. In a heap profile of the Microsoft Edge
browser process, which shares this code, about 231 KB was live under
GetThirdPartyInfo(), 200 KB of it in 383 map node allocations averaging
over 500 bytes each. With this change the map holds at most one entry
per third party, keyed by a short scheme://eTLD+1/ URL.

Adds the ThirdPartyMetricsObserverTest case
CookiesReadFromNonRootUrls_OneRecordedPerThirdParty, which reads cookies
from several non-root URLs of two third parties and expects CookieRead2
to record 2. Without the fix it records 3.

Bug: 568005397

"-heuristics" expectation files. Since the heuristics replace trailing
line breaks with spaces (crrev.com/c/8206664), trim trailing whitespace
from each extracted line in that mode.

tree
07e359a9c025f01d22391b2da9e6c5d551bf1241
parent
e3ff61d3cd1c5d3ff6c6d2e965397ff24c1bdea4 [ diff ] 
[Extensions] Fix DNRRedirectWithQueryAndRef flakiness

WebAccessibleResourcesBrowserTest.DNRRedirectWithQueryAndRef navigated
to example.com right after LoadExtension(). Static DNR rulesets are
loaded asynchronously by RulesMonitorService on the extension file task
runner after OnExtensionLoaded, and LoadExtension() does not wait for
them. If the main frame request reached the RulesetManager before the
ruleset was added, it was not redirected and NavigateToURL() failed to
commit the expected chrome-extension:// URL. This was most visible on
slower Android devices, where the test was disabled.

Wait for the ruleset with RulesetManagerObserver before navigating, as
other DNR browser tests do, and re-enable the test on Android. Also drop
the WaitForNavigationFinished() call after NavigateToURL(), which was a
no-op because NavigateToURL() already waits for the navigation.

0ce479b87e8fbecfc069b83bee8338963e540312

tree
c2f6dea82133e4ad91ae661ec8b442a20d293903
parent
d36c55760c0d67eb0028f4a8656024fde5954f7c [ diff ] 
ash: Fix TokenHandleChecker leak in TokenHandleStoreImpl::OnCheckToken

https://crrev.com/c/8448651 switched pending_checks_ cleanup to use
SequencedTaskRunner::DeleteSoon(). Because DeleteSoon() releases raw
pointer ownership before posting the task, the TokenHandleChecker is
leaked if the task queue is torn down before the deleter task runs (e.g.
at the end of unit tests, causing LSan failures in [1]).

Use PostTask() with base::DoNothingWithBoundArgs() instead so that the
std::unique_ptr retains ownership and destroys the checker even if the
posted task is dropped on shutdown.

[1]:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/b/8669319911968041281/test-results?q=ID:%3A%2F%2Fchrome%2Ftest%5C%3Aunit_tests!gtest%3A%3ATokenHandleStoreIsReauthRequiredTest%23IsReauthRequiredReturnsFalseIfTokenExistsAndStatusUnknown%20V:bucket=ci%20V:builder=Linux%20Chromium%20OS%20ASan%20LSan%20Tests%20(1)%20V:os=Ubuntu-22.04%20V:test_suite=unit_tests

ChromeOS Print Preview: Migrate to Ash shared elements

Desktop elements will be removing support for Polymer-based clients
in the near future. Since ChromeOS UIs use Polymer instead of Lit,
migrate the ChromeOS Print Preview to use the shared elements, styles
etc in ash/webui/common/ instead of the Desktop version in
ui/webui/resources. For the case of cr_scrollable, which doesn't
exist on ChromeOS, fork the style for CrOS Print Preview.

parent
ee454bec48f1feb3feaf8eeab53c94cd9a5d434b [ diff ] 
media: Improve Media.WatchTime.* UMA histogram descriptions

Previously, all 65 Media.WatchTime* histograms were generated from a
single patterned histogram (Media.WatchTime{MediaWatchTimeCategories})
where the variant summary was appended at the very end of the fourth
paragraph. This caused several readability and accuracy issues on the
UMA dashboard:

1. The variant-specific description blended into the final paragraph
   about seek events, making it hard to quickly tell what a specific
   metric reports.
2. The shared base summary assumed foreground, unmuted, audio+video
   watch time with a 7-second minimum, which directly contradicted:
   - .Audio.* variants (audio-only media),
   - .Background.* variants (recorded while hidden, stopped when shown),
   - .Muted.* variants (recorded while muted, stopped when unmuted), and
   - .Discarded.* variants (recorded when finalized with < 7s of watch
     time).
3. Several variant summaries had copy-paste typos ("with only with
   both", "with with both", and "MediaFoundations renderer").

This CL restructures Media.WatchTime* into 4 patterned histogram blocks
by recording mode (Foreground Unmuted, Background, Muted, and
Discarded), places each variant's specific summary in the first
paragraph, uses multi-token expansion ({TrackType} x {Category}) for
Background and Discarded variants, and fixes the typos and inaccuracies.

See the diff of old and new descriptions: go/diff/#key=SOHDGJvW49bK

The expanded set of 65 histogram names remains unchanged.

TAG=agy
CONV=c5b6eb2b-a027-42a4-90ec-12c539a4f8f8

Fix PointerEvent.twist range on Windows

On Windows, PointerEvent.twist was incorrectly limited to [0, 179]
because the rotation value was taken modulo 180.

0eb9f96002d58c0eb40d7e24fbac46c6fb69910b
parent
374ffba034cfd26f0a789255564d3818feeef48b [ diff ] 
[lens-overlay] Hide privacy notice for composebox button

Update `ShouldHideNonBlockingPrivacyNotice()` to return true when the
Lens Overlay is invoked from `kContextualTasksComposebox`.

When the user clicks the Lens button in the Contextual Tasks composebox,
the Contextual Tasks side panel is already open and the overlay
Contextual Searchbox (CSB) is hidden. Returning true in
`ShouldHideNonBlockingPrivacyNotice()` suppresses the CSB non-blocking
privacy notice UI, prevents incrementing the notice impression cap
counter, and skips recording privacy notice histograms while preserving
deferred session permission granting upon region selection.

Bug: b:565382823

parent
2f3e54592cba7cf9b6d8472d38f96f8c04069164 [ diff ] 
[glic] Set initial_invocation_source_ before logging initial instance events

GlicInstanceMetrics::LogEvent() only emits to the
Glic.InvocationSource.{InvocationSource}.EventCounts/HadEvent histograms
when initial_invocation_source_ is set. Currently, several initial
invocation flows log events before initial_invocation_source_ is set,
causing those events to be dropped from the per-source histograms (e.g.
kOpen, kTabBound). More details in bug.

This CL:
- Replaces `GlicInstanceMetrics::OnShowInactiveSidePanel()` with `GlicInstanceMetrics::MaybeSetInitialInvocationSource()` and routes all assignments of `initial_invocation_source_` through it.
- Reorders `GlicInstanceMetrics::OnOpen()` to set and log `initial_invocation_source_` before calling `LogEvent(kOpen)`.
- Calls `MaybeSetInitialInvocationSource()` at the start of `GlicInstanceImpl::Show()` and `GlicInvokeHandler::Invoke()`.
- Removes the now-redundant fallback in `GlicInstanceMetrics::OnShowInSidePanel()`.
- Updates unit and browser tests to verify that `kTabBound` and `kOpen` are recorded in `Glic.InvocationSource.{InvocationSource}.EventCounts` and `.HadEvent`.

Revert "Convert NetworkStateNotifier::NetworkStateObserver to GarbageCollectedMixin"

This reverts commit fbf9293efa4d87b473ef9cc9057e02a0b787ac5b.

Reason for revert: this CL causes  __main__.ChromeDriverTest.testEmulateNetworkConditionsOffline failures on Win10 Tests x64(dbg) and win11-arm64-dbg-tests.

https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/Win10%20Tests%20x64%20(dbg)/b8669102536904010113/overview

The stacktrace said that the crash seemed to be related to networkStateNotifier or NetworkStateObserver.

Original change's description:
> Convert NetworkStateNotifier::NetworkStateObserver to GarbageCollectedMixin
>
> NetworkStateNotifier::NetworkStateObserver is implemented by
> garbage-collected classes (such as NetworkInformation and
> LocalDOMWindow::NetworkStateObserver), while NetworkStateNotifier and
> NetworkStateObserverHandle hold untraced pointers to it. Holding a raw
> pointer to a non-GCed base of a GCed object is invisible to Oilpan and
> can lead to use-after-free if the GCed object is reclaimed.
>
> Convert NetworkStateNotifier::NetworkStateObserver to a
> GarbageCollectedMixin and store observers in NetworkStateNotifier using
> CrossThreadWeakHandle<NetworkStateObserver>. Key ObserverListMap by
> NetworkStateObserverHandle* mapping to an ObserverListEntry (holding the
> CrossThreadWeakHandle<NetworkStateObserver> and SingleThreadTaskRunner),
> and remove the now-redundant observer_ and task_runner_ fields from
> NetworkStateObserverHandle.
>

parent
a6569ddc277dab1e4c6fcef43c019271f7cf3843 [ diff ] 
[AAP] Disable PiP when activity is projected on DHU

After: https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NDY0MTAwMzk2ODM5NzMxMnxhNDg0YjU2OC05ZA

Regarding the change in ActivityTabWebContentsDelegateAndroid#isPictureInPictureEnabled:

mActivity.getApplicationContext() returns the single, global Application object shared by the entire Chrome process across all screens. Because there is only one Application object in memory, its deviceId and UI_MODE_TYPE (for Android 17+) flips back and forth between between the Phone screen (deviceId=0, UI_MODE_TYPE_NORMAL) and the DHU screen (deviceId=4, UI_MODE_TYPE_CAR), depending on which screen most recently touched it.

In contrast, mActivity represents one specific window on one specific screen:
  a) The Phone's Activity always has mActivity.getDeviceId() == 0.
  b) The DHU's Activity always has mActivity.getDeviceId() == 4 (my emulator).

[Context Hub] Open selected Smart Search results in a tab group

This CL introduces the ability to open selected URLs from Smart Search
results directly into a new tab group.

Additionally, it updates the conditions under which the
OpenUrlsInTabGroup mojo API can be accessed. It adds explicit validation
in ContextHubPageHandler to ensure the API is only executed when either
the kTopics or kSmartSearch feature flags are enabled, rejecting
requests if the conditions are violated.

[RTS] Enable RTS test skipping for chrome_public_unit_test_apk on android-10-x86-nofieldtrial-rel

This enables RTS (Regression Test Selection) filtering for
chrome_public_unit_test_apk on android-10-x86-nofieldtrial-rel (mirrored
by android-x86-rel and android-10-x86-nofieldtrial-rel try builders).

Potential savings:
~525 bot-hours daily (4 emulator shards, 71.0% of test time skipped).

[Vertical Tabs] Enable empty space context menu on new tab button

Enable the empty space context menu on the new tab button in Vertical
Tabs to match the horizontal tab strip behavior. Attach touch coordinate
tracking, context-click, and long-click listeners to R.id.new_tab_button
in VerticalTabListCoordinator, and handle keyboard-focused
R.id.new_tab_button in openKeyboardFocusedContextMenu().

https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NDY5ODI3ODQzMTAzMTI5NnwxMWU3ZThhOC01Mw

parent
d8a043fc4ffcadc60fd937f1772b2695cbc0b7cc [ diff ] 
[ios][web] Add Ensure{Block,Callback}Called(...) helper templates

WebKit has many assertions that terminate the application if the
completion handler it passes to the delegate has not been invoked
when they are deallocated.

To help ensure we call those completion handlers, add two templated
functions Ensure{Block,Callback}Called(...) that take respectively
a block or a callback and some default values and return a callback
that will invoke the wrapped object with the default values if it
is destroyed without being called. If the callback is invoked, then
the wrapped object is called with the corresponding values as usual.

This makes it easier to ensure that the completion handler passed by
WebKit are always invoked even if a PostTask(...) is dropped because
the application is shutting down or another reason prevents the code
from directly calling the completion handler.

59f2c008d6325e1fb90adb39a8f29d2ec01ee7fa [ diff ] 
settings: Require the mode argument on chrome://settings/cookies

CookieSettings explains one cookie mode, blocking or allowing
third-party cookies, and asserts unless its mode argument is
CookieControlsMode.BLOCK_THIRD_PARTY or INCOGNITO_ONLY. There is no
sensible mode to assume, since the page explains whichever option the
user tapped on the third-party cookies page.

Under SettingsInTabUrlNav the page is rebuilt from its URL, which is
user editable and replayed from history. crrev.com/c/8488318 registers
the argument as the typed "mode" query parameter so that it survives the
round trip, but the /cookies route still accepts a URL without a usable
mode: chrome://settings/cookies with "mode" missing, 0 or unparsable
(which parses to CookieControlsMode.OFF), or a URL written with the raw
cookie_page_state key before the parameter was registered, all reach
the page with mode 0. That asserts, and in release builds shows an empty
page.

Require the argument on the route and, when it is missing or not a mode
the page explains, redirect to the third-party cookies category, the
page /cookies is opened from and where both modes are offered.

parent
14ae9576c4499637959ff9f0ac7e96efef6bcb75 [ diff ] 
SidePanel: Rework auto-close/restore mechanism

This CL re-implements `OnWillAutoClose()` and `OnWillAutoRestore()` for
side panel to meet the expectation of `SideUiCoordinator`.

------------
The Issue
------------
(1) Per `SideUiCoordinator` design, `OnWillAutoClose()` and
    `OnWillAutoRestore()` shouldn't assume they can only be called when
    a change in available space causes side panel to close/open,
    although this is the typical case.

(2) `OnWillAutoClose()` and `OnWillAutoRestore()` must update side
    panel's internal states to match the "closed"/"opened" state.
    If `SideUiCoordinator` invokes either, side panel has already told
    `SideUiCoordinator` it should be closed/opened.

The previous code failed to implement (1) and (2), which can cause
crashes.

------------
Changes
------------
(1) `OnWillAutoClose()` and `OnWillAutoRestore()` will unconditionally
    close/open side panel.

(2) The "is_showable" parameter passed to `OnWillAutoClose()` can
    determine whether side panel is closed due to insufficient space,
    which is used to choose the right `SidePanelEntryHideReason`.

(3) `SidePanelDeferredEntryTracker` will track `DeferredReason` for each
    deferred entry. This allows us to choose the right
    `SidePanelOpenTrigger` in `OnWillAutoRestore()`.

Cq-Include-Trybots: luci.chromium.try:android-desktop-x64-rel
Include-Ci-Only-Tests: true

[ReadAloudNative][AIO] Pt4 - Add SetOverviewContent to Mojo interface

Add `SetOverviewContent` to the `ReadAloudPlaybackController` Mojo
interface in `chrome/common/readaloud/read_aloud.mojom` to pass
untrusted `ReadAloudGenerateTextResponse` bytes directly to the
sandboxed utility process for parsing and playback setup.

Background & Motivation: Instead of parsing complex protobufs
(`ReadAloudGenerateTextResponse`) in the privileged browser process and
re-serializing dialogue lines over Mojo as `TextSegment`s, we offload
deserialization and validation to the isolated
`ReadAloudPlaybackController` utility process. This aligns with the Rule
of 2 and initializes playback segments and prefetch caches directly in
the process where synthesis and playback occur.

IPC Details & Process Boundaries:
- Caller: Browser process (Privileged).
- Receiver: `ReadAloudPlaybackController` hosted in a Sandboxed Utility
    process.
- Interface:
    `SetOverviewContent(mojo_base.mojom.BigBuffer response_bytes)
        => (bool success, string title);`

Data Flow & Security Considerations:
- Data Origin: Untrusted binary payload (`ReadAloudGenerateTextResponse`
    proto bytes) received over the network from the remote GenAI/MES backend.
- Rule of 2 Compliance: Parsing and deserializing untrusted binary
    network payloads is shifted out of the privileged browser process and
    into a sandboxed utility process.
- Buffer Safety: Uses `mojo_base.mojom.BigBuffer` instead of inline byte
    vectors to prevent message size limitations and memory pressure on the
    Mojo IPC message channel for large responses.
- Validation & Error Handling: The utility process parses and validates
    the proto bytes, returning a fail-closed `success=false` if deserialization
    or validation fails. The browser only receives the generated title metadata and a success boolean.
- Current CL Scope: Includes the Mojo interface definition, a fail-closed
    stub in `ReadAloudPlaybackController`, and a mock implementation in
    `MockReadAloudPlaybackController`. Full proto parsing and playback setup
    follow in Part 4.5.

[Churned-User][Clank] Wire Message arm callbacks and snackbar

Connects user actions in the ResurrectChurnedUsers Android message
banner to the C++ callback lifecycle in
PaymentsChurnedUsersUiDelegateAndroid:
- Primary action click invokes closed_callback with kAccepted and shows
  the ResurrectChurnedUsers confirmation snackbar ("View settings") to
  open payment methods settings.
- Gesture dismissal (swiping the message away) invokes closed_callback
  with kCancelled, and timer/other dismissals invoke closed_callback
  with kNotInteracted.
- Guards against duplicate message prompts while a banner is active.

Video:
http://screencast/cast/NTE1Nzc2ODg2MzQxNjMyMHxmZGNmMDM4NS05ZQ

Design doc: go/autofill-clank-resurrection

base: Compare the pointer before the flag when a WeakPtr is matched

ObserverList::AddObserver() and RemoveObserver() scan the whole list to
find an observer. For checked observers each entry holds a
WeakPtr<CheckedObserver>, and CheckedObserverAdapter::IsEqual() called
WeakPtr::get(), which reads the WeakPtr's reference flag: one dependent
load into a separate heap allocation per entry. On a long list that is a
cache miss per entry, so a lookup in a list of ~5000 checked observers
took ~0.7 ms, against ~40 us for an Unchecked list of the same size,
whose entries are compared in place.

Long lists are common: profile-scoped services such as PrefService,
PasswordStore, MediaStreamCaptureIndicator or WebAppInstallManager get one
observer per tab from tab helpers, so adding N tabs costs O(N^2) lookups.
Restoring a session with 1555 tabs spent about 6 s in the AddObserver()
calls of seven such lists, and 2 s more in the four TracedWrapper
observers that every PerformanceManager page node registers.

Add operator== between a WeakPtr and a raw pointer (or raw_ptr) that
compares the stored pointer first and reads the reference flag only on a
match, and use it in CheckedObserverAdapter::IsEqual(). The result is
unchanged: an entry marked for removal (null WeakPtr) or whose observer
has been destroyed still compares unequal, and no extra state is stored
per entry. This shape was suggested by fdoray@ in crrev.com/c/8464187,
replacing an earlier version of this CL that cached the address in the
adapter.

The fast path only applies when the raw pointer is a pointer to T
itself. For any other pointer type the stored pointer would have to be
converted first, and converting a pointer to a destroyed object to one
of its virtual bases reads the freed memory, so those comparisons keep
going through get(). The fast path also keeps the sequence DCHECK that
get() performs, so a WeakPtr compared on the wrong sequence still fails
in DCHECK builds even when the pointers differ.

The new ObserverListPerfTest.AddRemovePerformance story adds and removes
one observer while the list holds N observers. On a fully optimized build
(non-component, DCHECKs off), per add+remove pair, three runs each:
  CheckedObserver, N=256:    0.75 us -> 0.16 us
  CheckedObserver, N=1024:   2.9-3.1 us -> 0.5-0.65 us
  CheckedObserver, N=4096:  11.2-13.5 us -> 2.0-2.1 us
  Unchecked, N=4096:         1.8-1.9 us (unchanged)
A checked list now scans about as fast as an unchecked one. The
microbenchmark keeps the WeakPtr flags cache-hot, so the gap there is
smaller than in the browser, where the flags of observers added over time
are scattered across the heap.

Measured on a component build (Windows), session restore of 1555 tabs,
three runs per variant (spread within 3%), measured with the earlier
version of this CL, which cached the address instead:
  tab insertion loop:                     35.5 s -> 22.9 s (-36%)
  TabHelpers::AttachTabHelpers, total:     8.8 s ->  4.7 s
  TabFeatures::Init, total:                6.5 s ->  2.8 s
  WebContents::Create, total:              6.2 s ->  2.4 s
  AddObserver() at ~1500 tabs (per call):  ~250-730 us -> ~20-35 us
  PageNodeImpl constructor at 1500 tabs:   2.4 ms -> 0.12 ms
  whole restore (launch to last tab):      60 s -> 45 s

[Actor] Backfill Glic conversation ID for Remy-initiated tasks

For Remy-initiated tasks, the Glic conversation ID is registered
asynchronously via GlicActorTaskManager::OnConversationRegistered()
after the native ActorTask and Java ActorTask wrapper have already been
created. As a result, ActorTask.getGlicConversationId() remained null
and clicking the task notification opened Glic in its default/new
conversation instead of the task's conversation.

This CL:
1. Backfills conversation_id onto pending native ActorTasks in
   GlicActorTaskManager::OnConversationRegistered() via SetSourceId().
2. Exposes ActorTaskAndroid::GetGlicConversationId() over JNI so Java
   ActorTask.getGlicConversationId() lazily fetches and caches the
   conversation ID when initially null.
3. Updates ActorNotificationFactory.shouldUpdateNotification() to return
   true when transitioning out of CREATED state so
   ActorNotificationService rebuilds the cached notification with the
   newly populated conversation ID and target tab ID.

TAG=agy
CONV=71c2ac87-85ad-4d0c-b9c5-bd178c46715d

[Glic] Enable Glic on foldables without the tablet flag

An unfolded foldable reports DEVICE_FORM_FACTOR_TABLET. As a result,
Glic is unavailable on expanded foldables unless the tablet flag is
enabled.

To fix this, allow the form factor when
base::android::device_info::is_foldable() is true, so foldables get Glic
in either form factor.

TAG=agy

overscroll: Switch to scroll snapshot and saved state.

Because the areas can change in response to style, which affects
other unrelated elements (specifically due to inertness), this patch
uses the last snapshot state to determine areas, which is updated
after style.

Another small fix here is to not restore the scroll offset for
areas. This means that when we reattach an area, it reattaches as
closed (no scroll is restored, overscrolling state is not restored)

R=flackr@chromium.org, futhark@chromium.org
